// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：値の正規化
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plPad_(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return (n < 10 ? "0" : "") + n; }
function plZen2Han_(s) {
  return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[０-９Ａ-Ｚａ-ｚ．，－：／％]/g, function (c) {
    return String.fromCharCode(c.charCodeAt(0) - 0xFEE0);
  });
}
function plNum_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return 0;
  if (typeof v === "number") return isFinite(v) ? v : 0;
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? 1 : 0;
  var s = plZen2Han_(String(v)).replace(/[,\s円¥￥%]/g, "");
  if (s === "" || s === "-") return 0;
  var n = Number(s);
  return isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}
function plIsDate_(v) { return Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]" && !isNaN(v.getTime()); }
// 日本時間の年月日・時分（Utilities に頼らないので Node でも同じ結果）
function plJstParts_(d) {
  var j = new Date(d.getTime() + 9 * 3600000);
  return { y: j.getUTCFullYear(), m: j.getUTCMonth() + 1, d: j.getUTCDate(), H: j.getUTCHours(), M: j.getUTCMinutes(), w: j.getUTCDay() };
}
function plValidYmd_(y, m, d) { return y >= 2000 && y <= 2100 && m >= 1 && m <= 12 && d >= 1 && d <= 31; }
// 日付 → "yyyy-MM-dd"（Date / "2026/10/2" / "2026-10-02" / "2026年10月2日" / シリアル値 / Date文字列）
function plDateKey_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return "";
  if (plIsDate_(v)) { var p = plJstParts_(v); return p.y + "-" + plPad_(p.m) + "-" + plPad_(p.d); }
  if (typeof v === "number") {
    if (v > 20000 && v < 80000) {
      var dt = new Date(Math.round((v - 25569) * 86400000));
      return dt.getUTCFullYear() + "-" + plPad_(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1) + "-" + plPad_(dt.getUTCDate());
    }
    return "";
  }
  var s = plZen2Han_(String(v)).trim();
  var m = s.match(/^(\d{4})\s*[\/\-年\.]\s*(\d{1,2})\s*[\/\-月\.]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
  if (m) return plValidYmd_(+m[1], +m[2], +m[3]) ? (m[1] + "-" + plPad_(+m[2]) + "-" + plPad_(+m[3])) : "";
  if (/[A-Za-z]{3}.*\d{4}/.test(s)) { var t = Date.parse(s); if (!isNaN(t)) return plDateKey_(new Date(t)); }
  return "";
}
// 年月 → "yyyy-MM"
function plMonthKey_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return "";
  if (plIsDate_(v)) { var p = plJstParts_(v); return p.y + "-" + plPad_(p.m); }
  var dk = plDateKey_(v); if (dk) return dk.slice(0, 7);
  var s = plZen2Han_(String(v)).trim();
  var m = s.match(/^(\d{4})\s*[\/\-年\.]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
  if (m && +m[2] >= 1 && +m[2] <= 12) return m[1] + "-" + plPad_(+m[2]);
  return "";
}
// 時刻 → 0時からの分（Date / "9:30" / "09:30:00" / 小数の日）
function plMinutes_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return null;
  if (plIsDate_(v)) { var p = plJstParts_(v); return p.H * 60 + p.M; }
  if (typeof v === "number") return (v >= 0 && v < 1) ? Math.round(v * 1440) : null;
  var s = plZen2Han_(String(v)).trim();
  var m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*[:時]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
  if (m) { var h = +m[1], mi = +m[2]; if (h < 48 && mi < 60) return h * 60 + mi; }
  return null;
}
// 出勤・退勤・休憩 → 実働の分
function plWorkMinutes_(ci, co, bs, be) {
  var a = plMinutes_(ci), b = plMinutes_(co);
  if (a === null || b === null || b <= a) return null;
  var x = plMinutes_(bs), y = plMinutes_(be);
  var brk = (x !== null && y !== null && y > x && x >= a && y <= b) ? (y - x) : 0;
  return Math.max(0, b - a - brk);
}
function plDaysInMonth_(ym) { var y = +ym.slice(0, 4), m = +ym.slice(5, 7); return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate(); }
function plAddDays_(dk, n) {
  var d = new Date(Date.UTC(+dk.slice(0, 4), +dk.slice(5, 7) - 1, +dk.slice(8, 10)) + n * 86400000);
  return d.getUTCFullYear() + "-" + plPad_(d.getUTCMonth() + 1) + "-" + plPad_(d.getUTCDate());
}
function plDow_(dk) { return new Date(Date.UTC(+dk.slice(0, 4), +dk.slice(5, 7) - 1, +dk.slice(8, 10))).getUTCDay(); }
function plDiffDays_(a, b) { // b - a（日）
  var x = Date.UTC(+a.slice(0, 4), +a.slice(5, 7) - 1, +a.slice(8, 10)), y = Date.UTC(+b.slice(0, 4), +b.slice(5, 7) - 1, +b.slice(8, 10));
  return Math.round((y - x) / 86400000);
}
// 期間内の営業日数（土日・祝日を除く。from・to を含む）
function plWeekdaysBetween_(from, to) {
  if (!from || !to || to < from) return 0;
  var n = 0, d = from, guard = 0;
  while (d <= to && guard < 4000) { var w = plDow_(d); if (w !== 0 && w !== 6 && !plHoliday_(d)) n++; d = plAddDays_(d, 1); guard++; }
  return n;
}
// v107: 日本の祝日（内閣府の規則：固定日・ハッピーマンデー・春分/秋分・振替休日・国民の休日）
var PL_HOL_CACHE_ = {};
function plNthMonday_(y, m, n) { var first = plDow_(y + "-" + plPad_(m) + "-01"); var d = 1 + ((8 - first) % 7) + (n - 1) * 7; return y + "-" + plPad_(m) + "-" + plPad_(d); }
function plJpHolidays_(y) {
  if (PL_HOL_CACHE_[y]) return PL_HOL_CACHE_[y];
  var h = {}, P = function (m, d) { return y + "-" + plPad_(m) + "-" + plPad_(d); };
  h[P(1, 1)] = "元日"; h[plNthMonday_(y, 1, 2)] = "成人の日"; h[P(2, 11)] = "建国記念の日";
  if (y >= 2020) h[P(2, 23)] = "天皇誕生日";
  var k = y - 1980;
  h[P(3, Math.floor(20.8431 + 0.242194 * k - Math.floor(k / 4)))] = "春分の日";
  h[P(4, 29)] = "昭和の日"; h[P(5, 3)] = "憲法記念日"; h[P(5, 4)] = "みどりの日"; h[P(5, 5)] = "こどもの日";
  h[plNthMonday_(y, 7, 3)] = "海の日"; h[P(8, 11)] = "山の日"; h[plNthMonday_(y, 9, 3)] = "敬老の日";
  h[P(9, Math.floor(23.2488 + 0.242194 * k - Math.floor(k / 4)))] = "秋分の日";
  h[plNthMonday_(y, 10, 2)] = "スポーツの日"; h[P(11, 3)] = "文化の日"; h[P(11, 23)] = "勤労感謝の日";
  var keys = Object.keys(h).sort();
  keys.forEach(function (d) {   // 振替休日：日曜の祝日 → 次の祝日でない日
    if (plDow_(d) !== 0) return;
    var x = plAddDays_(d, 1); while (h[x]) x = plAddDays_(x, 1); h[x] = "振替休日";
  });
  Object.keys(h).sort().forEach(function (d) {   // 国民の休日：祝日にはさまれた平日
    var x = plAddDays_(d, 2);
    if (h[x] && !h[plAddDays_(d, 1)] && plDow_(plAddDays_(d, 1)) !== 0) h[plAddDays_(d, 1)] = "国民の休日";
  });
  PL_HOL_CACHE_[y] = h; return h;
}
function plHoliday_(dk) { return dk ? (plJpHolidays_(+dk.slice(0, 4))[dk] || "") : ""; }
function plWeekdays_(ym) { return plWeekdaysBetween_(ym + "-01", ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym))); }
function plPersonKey_(s) { return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[\s\u3000]/g, ""); }

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：案件名の名寄せ（表記ゆれの吸収）
//   「(株)エヌケーティー」「株式会社エヌケーティー」「NKT」「ｴﾇｹｰﾃｨｰ」を同じ案件として扱う
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plNormName_(s) {
  var t = String(s == null ? "" : s);
  try { t = t.normalize("NFKC"); } catch (e) { t = plZen2Han_(t); }
  t = t.toLowerCase();
  t = t.replace(/株式会社|有限会社|合同会社|合資会社|合名会社|一般社団法人|一般財団法人|公益社団法人|公益財団法人|社会福祉法人|医療法人社団|医療法人財団|医療法人|特定非営利活動法人|npo法人|事業協同組合|協同組合/g, "");
  t = t.replace(/\((株|有|同|合|資|名|社|財|医|福|特非)\)/g, "");
  t = t.replace(/[()（）\[\]【】「」『』〈〉《》・,，.。、\s\-‐–—_\/／:：'"’”&＆]/g, "");
  return t;
}
function plSplitAliases_(s) {
  return String(s == null ? "" : s).split(/[｜|、,，\/／\n]/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(function (x) { return x; });
}
// 案件マスタの行 → 照合キーつき
function plPrepareProjects_(projects) {
  return (projects || []).map(function (p) {
    var keys = [plNormName_(p.name)].concat(plSplitAliases_(p.aliases).map(plNormName_));
    var seen = {};
    p.keys = keys.filter(function (k) { if (!k || seen[k]) return false; seen[k] = true; return true; });
    return p;
  });
}
// 英数字だけの語が、前後を英数字に挟まれずに含まれるか（"origin" の中の "rig" は不一致）
function plContainsWord_(hay, needle) {
  var from = 0, pos;
  while ((pos = hay.indexOf(needle, from)) >= 0) {
    var b = pos > 0 ? hay.charAt(pos - 1) : "", a = hay.charAt(pos + needle.length);
    if (!/[a-z0-9]/.test(b) && !/[a-z0-9]/.test(a)) return true;
    from = pos + 1;
  }
  return false;
}
// 案件名 → 案件（①正規化して完全一致 ②含む・含まれる。最長一致を採用）
function plMatchProject_(raw, projects) {
  var n = plNormName_(raw);
  if (!n) return null;
  var i, j, p;
  for (i = 0; i < projects.length; i++) {
    p = projects[i];
    for (j = 0; j < p.keys.length; j++) if (p.keys[j] === n) return p;
  }
  var best = null, bestLen = 0, nAscii = /^[a-z0-9]+$/.test(n);
  for (i = 0; i < projects.length; i++) {
    p = projects[i];
    for (j = 0; j < p.keys.length; j++) {
      var k = p.keys[j], hit = false;
      if (k.length < 2) continue;
      if (/^[a-z0-9]+$/.test(k)) hit = plContainsWord_(n, k);           // 英数字の別名：境界つき
      else hit = n.indexOf(k) >= 0;                                       // 日本語の別名：含めばよい
      if (!hit && !nAscii && n.length >= 3 && k.indexOf(n) >= 0) hit = true; // 報告が略称（例: 杵屋）
      if (hit && k.length > bestLen) { best = p; bestLen = k.length; }
    }
  }
  return best;
}
// アポ1件 → 売上の扱い
function plClassifyAppo_(a, projects, settings) {
  var unit = plNum_(settings.unitPrice) || 36000;
  var p = plMatchProject_(a.project, projects);
  if (p) {
    if (!p.revenue) return { pid: p.id, pname: p.name, counted: false, revenue: 0, registered: true };
    return { pid: p.id, pname: p.name, counted: true, revenue: p.price > 0 ? p.price : unit, registered: true };
  }
  var label = String(a.project || "").trim() || "(案件名なし)";
  return { pid: "", pname: label, counted: true, revenue: unit, registered: false };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：シートの値 → 正規化した行
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 勤怠 [名前, 日付, 出勤, 退勤, 休憩開始, 休憩終了]
function plNormAttendance_(vals) {
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < (vals || []).length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i], nm = plPersonKey_(r[0]), d = plDateKey_(r[1]);
    if (!nm || !d) continue;
    out.push({ name: nm, d: d, min: plWorkMinutes_(r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5]),
      hasIn: plMinutes_(r[2]) !== null, hasOut: plMinutes_(r[3]) !== null });
  }
  return out;
}
// 架電結果 [名前, 日付, 時刻, 会社名, 電話番号, 担当者, 結果, ...]
function plNormCalls_(vals) {
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < (vals || []).length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i], nm = plPersonKey_(r[0]), d = plDateKey_(r[1]);
    if (!nm || !d) continue;
    out.push({ name: nm, d: d, t: plMinutes_(r[2]), rid: mapResultLabelToId(r[6]) });
  }
  return out;
}
// アポイント [登録日, 報告者, 案件名, 商談日付, 商談時間, 法人名, ...]（「取消」列があれば除外）
function plNormAppos_(vals) {
  var out = [];
  if (!vals || !vals.length) return out;
  var head = vals[0] || [], cancelCol = -1;
  for (var c = 0; c < head.length; c++) { if (/取消|キャンセル/.test(String(head[c] || ""))) { cancelCol = c; break; } }
  for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i], d = plDateKey_(r[0]), rep = plPersonKey_(r[1]);
    if (!d) continue;
    var cx = cancelCol >= 0 ? String(r[cancelCol] || "").trim() : "";
    out.push({ row: i + 1, d: d, reporter: rep, project: String(r[2] || "").trim(), company: String(r[5] || "").trim(),
      cancelled: !!cx && !/^(no|いいえ|なし|false|0)$/i.test(cx), cancelNote: cx });
  }
  return out;
}
// シフト明細 [対象月, 日付, PIN, 名前, 開始, 終了, 休憩, 実働(分), 更新日時]
function plNormShifts_(vals) {
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < (vals || []).length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i], d = plDateKey_(r[1]);
    if (!d) continue;
    var pin = (typeof rosterNormPin_ === "function") ? rosterNormPin_(r[2]) : String(r[2] || "");
    out.push({ d: d, ym: d.slice(0, 7), pin: pin, name: plPersonKey_(r[3]), min: plNum_(r[7]) });
  }
  return out;
}
// シフト → {pin|ym: {days, min, byDay:{d:min}}}
function plShiftIndex_(shifts) {
  var idx = {};
  (shifts || []).forEach(function (s) {
    if (!s.pin) return;
    var k = s.pin + "|" + s.ym;
    if (!idx[k]) idx[k] = { days: 0, min: 0, byDay: {} };
    if (idx[k].byDay[s.d] === undefined) { idx[k].days++; idx[k].byDay[s.d] = 0; }
    idx[k].byDay[s.d] += s.min; idx[k].min += s.min;
  });
  return idx;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：報酬・所定日数
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 報酬の行（複数可）から、その月に有効なものを選ぶ（適用開始月が最も新しく、かつ対象月以前）
function plPayFor_(pays, pin, ym) {
  var mine = (pays || []).filter(function (p) { return p.pin === pin; });
  if (!mine.length) return { pin: pin, monthly: 0, hourly: 0, perAppo: 0, plannedDays: 0, from: "", missing: true };
  var ok = mine.filter(function (p) { return !p.from || p.from <= ym; });
  ok.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.from || "") < (b.from || "") ? -1 : 1; });
  if (ok.length) return ok[ok.length - 1];
  mine.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.from || "") < (b.from || "") ? -1 : 1; });
  return mine[0];
}
// 月給の日割りに使う「月の所定稼働日数」：報酬設定 → シフトの出勤日数 → 平日数
function plPlannedDays_(pay, shiftInfo, ym) {
  if (pay && pay.plannedDays > 0) return pay.plannedDays;
  if (shiftInfo && shiftInfo.days > 0) return shiftInfo.days;
  return plWeekdays_(ym) || 20;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：稼働（1人×1日）ごとの売上・投資コスト・損益
//   ctx = { from, to, members:[{pin,name,sh}], att, calls, appos(分類済み), pays, shiftIdx, settings }
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plSessions_(ctx) {
  var st = ctx.settings || {}, unit = plNum_(st.unitPrice) || 36000;
  var payExcluded = Number(st.commissionOnExcluded) !== 0;
  var byName = {};
  (ctx.members || []).forEach(function (m) {
    byName[plPersonKey_(m.name)] = m;
    if (m.sh && !byName[plPersonKey_(m.sh)]) byName[plPersonKey_(m.sh)] = m;
  });
  var slots = {};
  function memberOf(nm) { return byName[nm] || { pin: "", name: nm, sh: "", unknown: true }; }
  function slot(nm, d) {
    var m = memberOf(nm), k = (m.pin || ("?" + m.name)) + "|" + d;
    if (!slots[k]) slots[k] = { d: d, pin: m.pin, name: m.name, unknown: !!m.unknown, attMin: null, attRows: 0,
      calls: 0, connects: 0, callAppos: 0, tmin: null, tmax: null, appos: [], cancelled: 0, hasIn: false, hasOut: false };
    return slots[k];
  }
  function inRange(d) { return d >= ctx.from && d <= ctx.to; }
  (ctx.att || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (!inRange(r.d)) return;
    var s = slot(r.name, r.d); s.attRows++;
    if (r.hasIn) s.hasIn = true; if (r.hasOut) s.hasOut = true;
    if (r.min !== null && r.min !== undefined) s.attMin = Math.max(s.attMin || 0, r.min);
  });
  (ctx.calls || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (!inRange(r.d)) return;
    var s = slot(r.name, r.d); s.calls++;
    if (r.rid !== "absent" && r.rid !== "wrong") s.connects++;
    if (r.rid === "appo") s.callAppos++;
    if (r.t !== null && r.t !== undefined) {
      if (s.tmin === null || r.t < s.tmin) s.tmin = r.t;
      if (s.tmax === null || r.t > s.tmax) s.tmax = r.t;
    }
  });
  (ctx.appos || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (!inRange(a.d)) return;
    var s = slot(a.reporter || "(報告者なし)", a.d);
    if (a.cancelled) { s.cancelled++; return; }
    s.appos.push(a);
  });
  var out = [];
  Object.keys(slots).forEach(function (k) {
    var s = slots[k], ym = s.d.slice(0, 7);
    var pay = s.pin ? plPayFor_(ctx.pays, s.pin, ym) : { monthly: 0, hourly: 0, perAppo: 0, plannedDays: 0, missing: true };
    var hours, src;
    if (s.attMin !== null && s.attMin > 0) { hours = s.attMin / 60; src = "打刻"; }
    else if (s.tmin !== null && s.tmax !== null && s.tmax > s.tmin) { hours = (s.tmax - s.tmin) / 60; src = "推定"; }
    else { hours = 0; src = "なし"; }
    hours = Math.round(hours * 100) / 100;
    var revAppos = 0, nonRev = 0, revenue = 0, unreg = [];
    s.appos.forEach(function (a) {
      if (a.cls.counted) { revAppos++; revenue += a.cls.revenue; } else nonRev++;
      if (!a.cls.registered && unreg.indexOf(a.cls.pname) < 0) unreg.push(a.cls.pname);
    });
    var apposAll = revAppos + nonRev;
    var commissionAppos = payExcluded ? apposAll : revAppos;
    var info = ctx.shiftIdx ? ctx.shiftIdx[s.pin + "|" + ym] : null;
    var planned = plPlannedDays_(pay, info, ym);
    var costHourly = Math.round((pay.hourly || 0) * hours);
    var costCommission = (pay.perAppo || 0) * commissionAppos;
    var costFixed = pay.monthly > 0 ? Math.round(pay.monthly / planned) : 0;
    var cost = costHourly + costCommission + costFixed;
    var margin = unit - (pay.perAppo || 0);
    var flags = [];
    if (s.unknown) flags.push("名簿にない名前です（報酬が分からないためコスト0円）");
    else if (pay.missing) flags.push("報酬が未設定です（コスト0円で計算）");
    if (src === "推定") flags.push("退勤の打刻がないため、架電の最初〜最後で時間を推定");
    if (pay.hourly > 0 && hours === 0) flags.push("時給の方ですが実働時間がありません");
    if (s.callAppos !== apposAll) flags.push("架電結果のアポ" + s.callAppos + "件とアポ報告" + apposAll + "件が一致しません");
    if (unreg.length) flags.push("未登録の案件名: " + unreg.join("・"));
    out.push({
      d: s.d, pin: s.pin, name: s.name, hours: hours, hoursSrc: src, calls: s.calls, connects: s.connects,
      callAppos: s.callAppos, appos: apposAll, revAppos: revAppos, nonRevAppos: nonRev, cancelled: s.cancelled,
      revenue: revenue, costHourly: costHourly, costCommission: costCommission, costFixed: costFixed, cost: cost,
      profit: revenue - cost, roi: cost > 0 ? Math.round(revenue / cost * 1000) / 10 : null,
      bepAppos: margin > 0 ? Math.round(((costHourly + costFixed) / margin) * 10) / 10 : null,
      plannedDays: planned, flags: flags,
      // データ収集の点検：出勤打刻・退勤打刻・架電記録・アポ詳細の入力（架電結果と一致）・案件名がマスタと一致
      q: { inp: s.hasIn, out: s.hasOut, calls: s.calls > 0, apo: s.callAppos === apposAll, proj: unreg.length === 0 }
    });
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d === b.d ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1); });
  return out;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：直近90日の「1件あたり売上」と「売上対象の割合」（損益分岐の計算に使う）
//   月初はデータが少なく個人の数字がぶれるため、会社全体の実績を使う
function plMix_(appos, today, settings) {
  var unit = plNum_(settings.unitPrice) || 36000, since = plAddDays_(today, -89);
  var n = 0, rv = 0, cnt = 0;
  (appos || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (a.cancelled || !a.cls || a.d < since || a.d > today) return;
    n++; if (a.cls.counted) { cnt++; rv += a.cls.revenue; }
  });
  return { appos: n, revAppos: cnt, revenue: rv, since: since,
    revPerAppo: n > 0 ? rv / n : unit, countedShare: n > 0 ? cnt / n : 1 };
}
// 純粋関数：月の経過割合（平日ベース）と見込み
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plMonthProgress_(ym, asOf) {
  var start = ym + "-01", end = ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym));
  var total = plWeekdays_(ym) || 1;
  if (asOf >= end) return { start: start, end: end, total: total, elapsed: total, ratio: 1, done: true };
  if (asOf < start) return { start: start, end: end, total: total, elapsed: 0, ratio: 0, done: false };
  var el = plWeekdaysBetween_(start, asOf);
  return { start: start, end: end, total: total, elapsed: el, ratio: el / total, done: false };
}
// アポインター1人の月次まとめ（実績・月末見込み・損益分岐・KPI）
function plMemberMonth_(member, sessions, ym, asOf, ctx) {
  var st = ctx.settings || {}, unit = plNum_(st.unitPrice) || 36000;
  var payExcluded = Number(st.commissionOnExcluded) !== 0;
  var pay = plPayFor_(ctx.pays, member.pin, ym);
  var info = ctx.shiftIdx ? ctx.shiftIdx[member.pin + "|" + ym] : null;
  var planned = plPlannedDays_(pay, info, ym);
  var mine = sessions.filter(function (s) { return s.pin === member.pin && s.d.slice(0, 7) === ym; });
  var t = { days: 0, hours: 0, calls: 0, connects: 0, callAppos: 0, appos: 0, revAppos: 0, nonRevAppos: 0, revenue: 0,
    costHourly: 0, costCommission: 0, costFixedAlloc: 0 };
  var flags = {};
  mine.forEach(function (s) {
    t.days++; t.hours += s.hours; t.calls += s.calls; t.connects += s.connects; t.callAppos += s.callAppos;
    t.appos += s.appos; t.revAppos += s.revAppos; t.nonRevAppos += s.nonRevAppos; t.revenue += s.revenue;
    t.costHourly += s.costHourly; t.costCommission += s.costCommission; t.costFixedAlloc += s.costFixed;
    s.flags.forEach(function (f) { flags[f.replace(/\d+件/g, "◯件")] = true; });
  });
  t.hours = Math.round(t.hours * 100) / 100;
  var prog = plMonthProgress_(ym, asOf);
  // 月給：月の途中は「出勤した日の日割り」の合計。月が終わったら全額（出勤しなかった日の分も会社の費用）
  var costFixed = pay.monthly > 0 ? (prog.done ? pay.monthly : Math.min(t.costFixedAlloc, pay.monthly)) : 0;
  var fixedIdle = pay.monthly > 0 ? Math.max(0, costFixed - t.costFixedAlloc) : 0;
  var cost = t.costHourly + t.costCommission + costFixed;
  var ratio = prog.ratio > 0 ? 1 / prog.ratio : 0;
  // 時給の見込み：シフトがあれば「残りのシフト時間」、なければペースで延長
  var fHourly = t.costHourly;
  if (!prog.done) {
    if (info && info.min > 0 && pay.hourly > 0) {
      var remainMin = 0;
      Object.keys(info.byDay).forEach(function (d) { if (d > asOf) remainMin += info.byDay[d]; });
      fHourly = t.costHourly + Math.round(pay.hourly * remainMin / 60);
    } else fHourly = Math.round(t.costHourly * ratio);
  }
  var fAppos = prog.done ? t.appos : Math.round(t.appos * ratio * 10) / 10;
  var fRevenue = prog.done ? t.revenue : Math.round(t.revenue * ratio);
  var fCommission = prog.done ? t.costCommission : Math.round(t.costCommission * ratio);
  var fFixed = pay.monthly || 0;
  var fCost = fHourly + fCommission + fFixed;
  // 損益分岐（CVP）：固定的な費用 ÷ 1件あたりの限界利益
  //   1件あたり売上は「直近90日の会社全体の実績」（対象外案件の割合を反映）
  var mix = ctx.mix || { revPerAppo: unit, countedShare: 1 };
  var avgRev = mix.revPerAppo;
  var commPerAppo = (pay.perAppo || 0) * (payExcluded ? 1 : mix.countedShare);
  var margin = avgRev - commPerAppo;
  var fixedish = fFixed + fHourly;
  var need = margin > 0 ? Math.ceil(fixedish / margin - 1e-9) : null;
  var bep = {
    fixedCost: fixedish, marginPerAppo: Math.round(margin), needAppos: need,
    remaining: need === null ? null : Math.max(0, need - t.appos), reached: need !== null && t.appos >= need,
    hoursPerAppo: (pay.hourly > 0 && margin > 0) ? Math.round(margin / pay.hourly * 10) / 10 : null
  };
  // KPI（既存の目標値を使う）
  var kpi = null;
  try {
    var targets = ctx.kpiTargets || {};
    var tg = targets[member.pin] || (typeof KPI_DEFAULT !== "undefined" ? KPI_DEFAULT : { calls: 60, connRate: 30, apoRate: 3, apos: 2, hours: 6 });
    var days = mine.map(function (s) { return { calls: s.calls, connects: s.connects, appos: s.appos, hours: s.hours }; });
    kpi = kpiEvaluate_(days, tg);
    kpi.target = tg;
  } catch (eK) { kpi = null; }
  var roiTarget = plNum_(st.roiTarget) || 200;
  var roi = cost > 0 ? Math.round(t.revenue / cost * 1000) / 10 : null;
  return {
    pin: member.pin, name: member.name, ym: ym,
    pay: { monthly: pay.monthly || 0, hourly: pay.hourly || 0, perAppo: pay.perAppo || 0, from: pay.from || "", missing: !!pay.missing,
      plannedDays: pay.plannedDays || 0 },
    plannedDays: planned, days: t.days, hours: t.hours, calls: t.calls, connects: t.connects, callAppos: t.callAppos,
    appos: t.appos, revAppos: t.revAppos, nonRevAppos: t.nonRevAppos, revenue: t.revenue,
    costHourly: t.costHourly, costCommission: t.costCommission, costFixed: costFixed, fixedIdle: fixedIdle, cost: cost,
    profit: t.revenue - cost, roi: roi, roiTarget: roiTarget,
    cpa: t.appos > 0 ? Math.round(cost / t.appos) : null,
    revPerHour: t.hours > 0 ? Math.round(t.revenue / t.hours) : null,
    costPerHour: t.hours > 0 ? Math.round(cost / t.hours) : null,
    forecast: { appos: fAppos, revenue: fRevenue, costHourly: fHourly, costCommission: fCommission, costFixed: fFixed,
      cost: fCost, profit: fRevenue - fCost },
    bep: bep, kpi: kpi, flags: Object.keys(flags)
  };
}
// 会社全体（チーム）の損益と損益分岐
function plTeam_(members, ctx, ym, asOf) {
  var st = ctx.settings || {}, unit = plNum_(st.unitPrice) || 36000;
  var other = plNum_(st.otherFixedMonthly);
  var prog = plMonthProgress_(ym, asOf);
  var T = { revenue: 0, cost: 0, appos: 0, revAppos: 0, hours: 0, calls: 0, commission: 0,
    fRevenue: 0, fCost: 0, fAppos: 0, fixedish: 0, fCommission: 0 };
  members.forEach(function (m) {
    T.revenue += m.revenue; T.cost += m.cost; T.appos += m.appos; T.revAppos += m.revAppos; T.hours += m.hours; T.calls += m.calls;
    T.commission += m.costCommission;
    T.fRevenue += m.forecast.revenue; T.fCost += m.forecast.cost; T.fAppos += m.forecast.appos;
    T.fixedish += m.forecast.costFixed + m.forecast.costHourly; T.fCommission += m.forecast.costCommission;
  });
  var otherToDate = Math.round(other * prog.ratio);
  var cost = T.cost + otherToDate, fCost = T.fCost + other;
  var mix = ctx.mix || { revPerAppo: unit, countedShare: 1 };
  var avgRev = mix.revPerAppo;
  var commAvg;
  if (T.appos > 0) commAvg = T.commission / T.appos;
  else {
    var per = members.map(function (m) { return m.pay.perAppo || 0; });
    commAvg = per.length ? per.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / per.length : 0;
  }
  var margin = avgRev - commAvg;
  var fixedTotal = T.fixedish + other;
  var need = margin > 0 ? Math.ceil(fixedTotal / margin - 1e-9) : null;
  var bepRevenue = need !== null ? Math.round(need * avgRev) : null;
  var bepRatio = (bepRevenue !== null && T.fRevenue > 0) ? Math.round(bepRevenue / T.fRevenue * 1000) / 10 : null;
  var xmax = Math.max(5, Math.ceil(Math.max(need || 0, T.fAppos, T.appos) * 1.3));
  return {
    ym: ym, progress: prog, revenue: T.revenue, cost: cost, profit: T.revenue - cost,
    roi: cost > 0 ? Math.round(T.revenue / cost * 1000) / 10 : null,
    appos: T.appos, revAppos: T.revAppos, hours: Math.round(T.hours * 100) / 100, calls: T.calls,
    otherFixed: other, otherToDate: otherToDate,
    forecast: { revenue: T.fRevenue, cost: fCost, profit: T.fRevenue - fCost, appos: Math.round(T.fAppos * 10) / 10 },
    mix: { revPerAppo: Math.round(mix.revPerAppo), countedShare: Math.round((mix.countedShare || 0) * 1000) / 10, appos: mix.appos || 0, since: mix.since || "" },
    bep: { fixedCost: fixedTotal, avgRevPerAppo: Math.round(avgRev), commissionPerAppo: Math.round(commAvg),
      marginPerAppo: Math.round(margin), needAppos: need, remaining: need === null ? null : Math.max(0, need - T.appos),
      reached: need !== null && T.appos >= need, bepRevenue: bepRevenue, bepRatio: bepRatio,
      safety: bepRatio === null ? null : Math.round((100 - bepRatio) * 10) / 10, xmax: xmax }
  };
}
// 日別の会社合計（グラフ用）
function plDailySeries_(sessions, ym, asOf) {
  var by = {};
  sessions.forEach(function (s) {
    if (s.d.slice(0, 7) !== ym) return;
    if (!by[s.d]) by[s.d] = { d: s.d, revenue: 0, cost: 0, appos: 0, hours: 0 };
    by[s.d].revenue += s.revenue; by[s.d].cost += s.cost; by[s.d].appos += s.appos; by[s.d].hours += s.hours;
  });
  var out = [], cum = 0, last = ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym));
  var end = asOf < last ? asOf : last;
  for (var d = ym + "-01"; d <= end; d = plAddDays_(d, 1)) {
    var x = by[d] || { d: d, revenue: 0, cost: 0, appos: 0, hours: 0 };
    cum += x.revenue - x.cost;
    out.push({ d: d, revenue: x.revenue, cost: x.cost, profit: x.revenue - x.cost, cum: cum, appos: x.appos,
      hours: Math.round(x.hours * 100) / 100 });
  }
  return out;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：案件ごとの消化度合い・残りアポ数（バーンダウン）
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plProjects_(projects, appos, today) {
  var ym = today.slice(0, 7);
  var since14 = plAddDays_(today, -13);
  return (projects || []).map(function (p) {
    var monthly = p.scope === "毎月";
    var from = monthly ? (ym + "-01") : (p.start || "");
    var deadline = monthly ? (ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym))) : (p.end || "");
    var got = 0, recent = 0, cancelled = 0, last = "";
    (appos || []).forEach(function (a) {
      if (!a.cls || a.cls.pid !== p.id) return;
      if (from && a.d < from) return;
      if (a.d > today) return;
      if (a.cancelled) { cancelled++; return; }
      got++;
      if (a.d >= since14) recent++;
      if (a.d > last) last = a.d;
    });
    var req = p.required || 0;
    var remaining = req > 0 ? Math.max(0, req - got) : null;
    var daysLeft = deadline ? plWeekdaysBetween_(today, deadline) : null;
    var pace = Math.round(recent / Math.max(1, plWeekdaysBetween_(since14, today)) * 100) / 100;
    var needPerDay = (remaining !== null && daysLeft) ? Math.round(remaining / daysLeft * 100) / 100 : null;
    var forecastDate = "";
    if (remaining && pace > 0) {
      var need = Math.ceil(remaining / pace), d = today, n = 0, g = 0;
      while (n < need && g < 1500) { d = plAddDays_(d, 1); var w = plDow_(d); if (w !== 0 && w !== 6) n++; g++; }
      forecastDate = d;
    }
    // 理想線：開始日〜期限を一定ペースで消化したときの「今日の位置」
    var ideal = null;
    if (req > 0 && deadline) {
      var st0 = from || today;
      var tot = plWeekdaysBetween_(st0, deadline), el = plWeekdaysBetween_(st0, today);
      if (tot > 0) ideal = Math.min(req, Math.round(req * el / tot * 10) / 10);
    }
    var status;
    if (p.status === "完了" || p.status === "停止") status = p.status;
    else if (!req) status = "必要数が未設定";
    else if (remaining === 0) status = "達成";
    else if (deadline && today > deadline) status = "期限超過";
    else if (needPerDay !== null && pace >= needPerDay) status = "順調";
    else if (needPerDay !== null && pace >= needPerDay * 0.7) status = "注意";
    else if (needPerDay === null) status = pace > 0 ? "進行中" : "動きなし";
    else status = "遅れ";
    return { id: p.id, name: p.name, aliases: p.aliases, revenue: p.revenue, price: p.price, required: req,
      scope: monthly ? "毎月" : "総数", start: p.start, end: p.end, state: p.status, note: p.note,
      from: from, deadline: deadline, acquired: got, remaining: remaining, cancelled: cancelled,
      pct: req > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round(got / req * 1000) / 10) : null,
      daysLeft: daysLeft, needPerDay: needPerDay, pace: pace, forecastDate: forecastDate, ideal: ideal,
      lastAppo: last, status: status };
  });
}
// 案件マスタに無い案件名（名寄せ漏れ）の一覧
function plUnregistered_(appos, sinceKey) {
  var by = {};
  (appos || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (!a.cls || a.cls.registered || a.cancelled || (sinceKey && a.d < sinceKey)) return;
    var k = plNormName_(a.project) || "(案件名なし)";
    if (!by[k]) by[k] = { name: a.cls.pname, count: 0, last: "", spell: {} };
    by[k].count++; if (a.d > by[k].last) by[k].last = a.d;
    by[k].spell[a.cls.pname] = (by[k].spell[a.cls.pname] || 0) + 1;
  });
  // 表示名は「いちばん多い書き方」、ほかの書き方は別名の候補として返す
  return Object.keys(by).map(function (k) {
    var x = by[k], names = Object.keys(x.spell).sort(function (a, b) { return x.spell[b] - x.spell[a] || (a < b ? -1 : 1); });
    return { name: names[0], count: x.count, last: x.last, variants: names.slice(1) };
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.count - a.count; });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 純粋関数：今期（決算月から算出）・売上台帳・回収
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plFiscal_(todayKey, endMonth) {
  var em = Math.min(12, Math.max(1, Math.round(plNum_(endMonth) || 2)));
  var y = +todayKey.slice(0, 4), m = +todayKey.slice(5, 7);
  var sm = em % 12 + 1;
  var sy = (m >= sm) ? y : y - 1;
  var months = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    var mm = sm + i, yy = sy + Math.floor((mm - 1) / 12);
    mm = (mm - 1) % 12 + 1;
    months.push(yy + "-" + plPad_(mm));
  }
  var endYm = months[11];
  return { start: months[0] + "-01", end: endYm + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(endYm)), months: months,
    label: months[0].slice(0, 4) + "年度（" + endYm.slice(0, 4) + "年" + em + "月期）", endMonth: em };
}
// 入金期日：明記があればそれ。なければ「翌月末」または「請求日＋n日」
function plDueDate_(invoiceDate, ym, rule) {
  var base = invoiceDate || (ym ? ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym)) : "");
  if (!base) return "";
  var r = String(rule == null ? "" : rule).trim();
  var n = plNum_(r);
  if (n > 0 && /^[\d０-９]+$/.test(plZen2Han_(r))) return plAddDays_(base, n);
  var y = +base.slice(0, 4), m = +base.slice(5, 7) + 1;
  if (m > 12) { m = 1; y++; }
  var nym = y + "-" + plPad_(m);
  return nym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(nym));
}
function plLedgerStatus_(billed, paid, dueDate, today) {
  var out = billed - paid;
  var overdue = out > 0 && dueDate && today > dueDate ? plDiffDays_(dueDate, today) : 0;
  var status;
  if (billed <= 0) status = "—";
  else if (out <= 0) status = "入金済";
  else if (paid > 0) status = overdue ? "一部入金・期日超過" : "一部入金";
  else status = overdue ? "期日超過" : "未入金";
  return { status: status, outstanding: Math.max(0, out), overdueDays: overdue };
}
// 売上台帳の行（見出しつき）→ 正規化
function plNormLedger_(vals, settings, today) {
  var out = [], rate0 = plNum_(settings.taxRate) || 10;
  for (var i = 1; i < (vals || []).length; i++) {
    var r = vals[i], id = String(r[0] || "").trim();
    if (!id) continue;
    var ym = plMonthKey_(r[1]);
    var amount = plNum_(r[4]);
    var rate = (r[5] === "" || r[5] === null || r[5] === undefined) ? rate0 : plNum_(r[5]);
    var billed = plNum_(r[6]) || Math.round(amount * (1 + rate / 100));
    var inv = plDateKey_(r[7]);
    var due = plDateKey_(r[8]) || plDueDate_(inv, ym, settings.dueRule);
    var paid = plNum_(r[9]);
    var stt = plLedgerStatus_(billed, paid, due, today);
    out.push({ row: i + 1, id: id, ym: ym, client: String(r[2] || "").trim(), desc: String(r[3] || "").trim(),
      amount: amount, taxRate: rate, billed: billed, invoiceDate: inv, dueDate: due, dueAuto: !plDateKey_(r[8]),
      paid: paid, paidDate: plDateKey_(r[10]), status: stt.status, outstanding: stt.outstanding, overdueDays: stt.overdueDays,
      note: String(r[12] || ""), source: String(r[13] || "") });
  }
  return out;
}
// 今期の売上・目標との差・回収状況
function plFySummary_(ledger, fy, today, target, appoRevByMonth, bankSalesNet, basis) {
  bankSalesNet = bankSalesNet || {}; basis = basis || "台帳";
  var months = fy.months.map(function (ym) { return { ym: ym, sales: 0, ledgerSales: 0, bankSales: bankSalesNet[ym] || 0, basis: "", appoRev: appoRevByMonth[ym] || 0, billed: 0, paid: 0 }; });
  var idx = {}; months.forEach(function (m, i) { idx[m.ym] = i; });
  var inFy = (ledger || []).filter(function (r) { return idx[r.ym] !== undefined; });
  inFy.forEach(function (r) { var m = months[idx[r.ym]]; m.ledgerSales += r.amount; m.billed += r.billed; m.paid += Math.min(r.paid, r.billed); });
  months.forEach(function (m) {
    if (basis === "銀行") { m.sales = m.bankSales; m.basis = m.bankSales ? "銀行" : ""; }
    else if (basis === "自動" && !m.ledgerSales && m.bankSales) { m.sales = m.bankSales; m.basis = "銀行"; }
    else { m.sales = m.ledgerSales; m.basis = m.ledgerSales ? "台帳" : ""; }
  });
  var cum = 0, cumAppo = 0;
  months.forEach(function (m, i) { cum += m.sales; cumAppo += m.appoRev; m.cum = cum; m.cumAppo = cumAppo; m.targetCum = Math.round(target * (i + 1) / 12); });
  var actual = cum;
  var ty = today.slice(0, 7), k = idx[ty];
  var elapsed;
  if (today > fy.end) elapsed = 12;
  else if (today < fy.start) elapsed = 0;
  else elapsed = k + (+today.slice(8, 10)) / plDaysInMonth_(ty);
  // 着地見込み：台帳の実績 ＋ 当月（台帳が空ならアポ実績の月末見込み）＋ 締めた月の平均 × 当月より後の月数
  var closed = months.filter(function (m) { return m.ym < ty; });
  var closedWithSales = closed.filter(function (m) { return m.sales > 0; });
  var avgClosed = closedWithSales.length ? closedWithSales.reduce(function (a, m) { return a + m.sales; }, 0) / closedWithSales.length : 0;
  var cur = (k !== undefined) ? months[k] : null;
  var curExtra = 0;
  if (cur && cur.sales <= 0) {
    var dim = plDaysInMonth_(ty), dd = +today.slice(8, 10);
    curExtra = Math.round(cur.appoRev * dim / Math.max(1, dd));
  }
  if (!avgClosed && cur) avgClosed = cur.sales > 0 ? cur.sales : curExtra;
  var after = (k !== undefined) ? (11 - k) : (today < fy.start ? 12 : 0);
  var landing = Math.round(actual + curExtra + avgClosed * after);
  var landingAppo = elapsed > 0 ? Math.round(cumAppo / elapsed * 12) : 0;
  var remainMonths = Math.max(0, 12 - elapsed);
  var gap = target - actual;
  // 回収（今期分）
  var col = { billed: 0, paid: 0, outstanding: 0, overdue: 0, rows: inFy.length };
  inFy.forEach(function (r) { col.billed += r.billed; col.paid += Math.min(r.paid, r.billed); col.outstanding += r.outstanding; if (r.overdueDays > 0) col.overdue += r.outstanding; });
  col.rate = col.billed > 0 ? Math.round(col.paid / col.billed * 1000) / 10 : null;
  // 未回収の年齢表（全期間）
  var aging = { notDue: 0, d1_30: 0, d31_60: 0, d61: 0, total: 0 };
  var byClient = {};
  (ledger || []).forEach(function (r) {
    if (r.outstanding <= 0) return;
    aging.total += r.outstanding;
    if (r.overdueDays <= 0) aging.notDue += r.outstanding;
    else if (r.overdueDays <= 30) aging.d1_30 += r.outstanding;
    else if (r.overdueDays <= 60) aging.d31_60 += r.outstanding;
    else aging.d61 += r.outstanding;
    var c = r.client || "(取引先なし)";
    if (!byClient[c]) byClient[c] = { client: c, outstanding: 0, overdue: 0, maxDays: 0 };
    byClient[c].outstanding += r.outstanding;
    if (r.overdueDays > 0) { byClient[c].overdue += r.outstanding; byClient[c].maxDays = Math.max(byClient[c].maxDays, r.overdueDays); }
  });
  return {
    fy: fy, target: target, actual: actual, achievement: target > 0 ? Math.round(actual / target * 1000) / 10 : null,
    gap: gap, elapsedMonths: Math.round(elapsed * 100) / 100, remainMonths: Math.round(remainMonths * 100) / 100,
    needPerMonth: (gap > 0 && remainMonths > 0) ? Math.round(gap / remainMonths) : 0,
    basis: basis, ledgerTotal: months.reduce(function (a, m) { return a + m.ledgerSales; }, 0), bankTotal: months.reduce(function (a, m) { return a + m.bankSales; }, 0),
    landing: landing, landingGap: target - landing, landingAppo: landingAppo, appoRevTotal: cumAppo,
    landingBasis: { ledger: actual, currentFromAppos: curExtra, avgMonthly: Math.round(avgClosed), monthsAfter: after },
    months: months, collection: col, aging: aging,
    byClient: Object.keys(byClient).map(function (c) { return byClient[c]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.outstanding - a.outstanding; })
  };
}
// アポ実績 → 月の売上台帳の行（案件ごと）。IDは「A-年月-案件」で固定（再実行しても増えない）
function plLedgerDraftFromAppos_(appos, ym) {
  var by = {};
  (appos || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (a.cancelled || !a.cls || !a.cls.counted || a.d.slice(0, 7) !== ym) return;
    var key = a.cls.pid || ("U-" + (plNormName_(a.cls.pname) || "none"));
    if (!by[key]) by[key] = { id: "A-" + ym + "-" + key, ym: ym, client: a.cls.pname, count: 0, amount: 0 };
    by[key].count++; by[key].amount += a.cls.revenue;
  });
  return Object.keys(by).sort().map(function (k) {
    var x = by[k]; x.desc = "アポイント " + x.count + "件（アプリ集計）"; return x;
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// シート入出力
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
var PL_BOOK_CACHE_ = null;
function plBook_() {
  if (PL_BOOK_CACHE_) return PL_BOOK_CACHE_;
  try { PL_BOOK_CACHE_ = { ss: SpreadsheetApp.openById(ADMIN_SHEET_ID), where: "管理者用スプレッドシート" }; }
  catch (e) { PL_BOOK_CACHE_ = { ss: SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID), where: "メインスプレッドシート（管理者用が開けないため）" }; }
  return PL_BOOK_CACHE_;
}
function plSheetVals_(ss, name) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  return (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 0) ? sh.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
}
function plStyleHeader_(sh, n) {
  try { sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, n).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e0f2fe"); sh.setFrozenRows(1); } catch (e) {}
}
// 必要なシートを用意（初回だけ既定値を書き込む）
function plEnsure_() {
  var ss = plBook_().ss;
  function mk(name, header, rows) {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
    if (sh) return sh;
    sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, header.length).setValues([header]);
    plStyleHeader_(sh, header.length);
    if (rows && rows.length) {
      var now = new Date();
      var data = rows.map(function (r) {
        var x = r.slice(0, header.length - 1);
        while (x.length < header.length - 1) x.push("");
        x.push(now);
        return x;
      });
      sh.getRange(2, 1, data.length, header.length).setValues(data);
    }
    return sh;
  }
  mk(PL_SHEET.settings, PL_SETTINGS_HEADER, PL_SETTINGS_DEFAULT);
  mk(PL_SHEET.pay, PL_PAY_HEADER, PL_PAY_DEFAULT.map(function (r) { var x = r.slice(); x[0] = "'" + x[0]; return x; }));
  mk(PL_SHEET.project, PL_PROJECT_HEADER, PL_PROJECT_DEFAULT);
  mk(PL_SHEET.ledger, PL_LEDGER_HEADER, []);
  mk(PL_SHEET.daily, PL_DAILY_HEADER, []);
  mk(PL_SHEET.bank, PL_BANK_HEADER, []);
  mk(PL_SHEET.bankRule, PL_RULE_HEADER, PL_RULE_DEFAULT.map(function (r) { var x = r.slice(); if (x[3]) x[3] = asText_(x[3]); return x; }));   // v108: PINは文字列で
  // 設定項目が後から増えた場合に補う
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.settings);
  var have = {}; plSheetVals_(ss, PL_SHEET.settings).forEach(function (r, i) { if (i) have[String(r[0])] = true; });
  var add = PL_SETTINGS_DEFAULT.filter(function (r) { return !have[r[0]]; });
  if (add.length) sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, add.length, PL_SETTINGS_HEADER.length)
    .setValues(add.map(function (r) { return [r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], new Date()]; }));
  return ss;
}
function plReadSettings_(ss) {
  var o = {};
  PL_SETTINGS_DEFAULT.forEach(function (r) { o[r[0]] = r[2]; });
  plSheetVals_(ss, PL_SHEET.settings).forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!i) return;
    var k = String(r[0] || "").trim(); if (!k) return;
    if (r[2] !== "" && r[2] !== null && r[2] !== undefined) o[k] = PL_TEXT_SETTINGS[k] ? String(r[2]).trim() : plNum_(r[2]);
  });
  return o;
}
function plReadPays_(ss) {
  var out = [];
  plSheetVals_(ss, PL_SHEET.pay).forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!i) return;
    var pin = rosterNormPin_(r[0]); if (!pin) return;
    if (!pinOwnerOk_(pin, r[1])) return;   // v108: 同じPINの別人（以前の在籍者）の報酬は使わない
    out.push({ pin: pin, name: String(r[1] || ""), from: plMonthKey_(r[2]), monthly: plNum_(r[3]), hourly: plNum_(r[4]),
      perAppo: plNum_(r[5]), plannedDays: plNum_(r[6]), note: String(r[7] || ""), row: i + 1 });
  });
  return out;
}
function plReadProjects_(ss) {
  var out = [];
  plSheetVals_(ss, PL_SHEET.project).forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!i) return;
    var name = String(r[1] || "").trim(); if (!name) return;
    var rv = String(r[3] == null ? "" : r[3]).trim();
    out.push({ id: String(r[0] || "").trim() || ("P-" + (i + 1)), name: name, aliases: String(r[2] || ""),
      revenue: !/^(しない|いいえ|no|false|0|×|対象外)$/i.test(rv), price: plNum_(r[4]), required: Math.round(plNum_(r[5])),
      scope: /毎月|月/.test(String(r[6] || "")) ? "毎月" : "総数", start: plDateKey_(r[7]), end: plDateKey_(r[8]),
      status: String(r[9] || "進行中").trim() || "進行中", note: String(r[10] || ""), row: i + 1 });
  });
  return plPrepareProjects_(out);
}
function plMembers_() {
  var out = [], seen = {};
  Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (p) { seen[p] = true; out.push({ pin: p, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[p], sh: PIN_MAP[p] || "", active: true }); });
  try {
    (rosterLoad_(false).all || []).forEach(function (m) {
      if (seen[m.pin]) return; seen[m.pin] = true;
      out.push({ pin: m.pin, name: m.name, sh: m.sh || "", active: false });
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.active === b.active ? (a.pin < b.pin ? -1 : 1) : (a.active ? -1 : 1); });   // 並びはPIN順（毎回同じ）
  return out;
}
function plTodayKey_() { return plDateKey_(new Date()); }

// 計算に必要な材料をまとめて読む（1回の要求でシートを1度だけ読む）
function plLoad_() {
  var book = plBook_(), bss = plEnsure_();
  var main = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var settings = plReadSettings_(bss);
  var projects = plReadProjects_(bss);
  var appos = plNormAppos_(plSheetVals_(main, "アポイント"));
  appos.forEach(function (a) { a.cls = plClassifyAppo_(a, projects, settings); });
  var kt = {};
  try { kt = kpiTargetsRead_(); } catch (e) {}
  return {
    where: book.where, settings: settings, pays: plReadPays_(bss), projects: projects, appos: appos,
    att: plNormAttendance_(plSheetVals_(main, "勤怠")), calls: plNormCalls_(plSheetVals_(main, "架電結果")),
    shiftIdx: plShiftIndex_(plNormShifts_(plSheetVals_(main, "シフト明細"))),
    ledgerVals: plSheetVals_(bss, PL_SHEET.ledger), members: plMembers_(), kpiTargets: kt,
    bankVals: plSheetVals_(bss, PL_SHEET.bank), ruleVals: plSheetVals_(bss, PL_SHEET.bankRule)
  };
}

// ★管理者ダッシュボード：月を指定して、全部をまとめて返す
function plDashboard(month, todayOpt) {
  var today = plDateKey_(todayOpt) || plTodayKey_();
  var ym = plMonthKey_(month) || today.slice(0, 7);
  var L = plLoad_();
  var monthEnd = ym + "-" + plPad_(plDaysInMonth_(ym));
  var asOf = today < monthEnd ? today : monthEnd;
  var fy = plFiscal_(today, L.settings.fiscalEndMonth);
  // v107: 銀行明細を先に集計 → その他の固定費（損益分岐）と今期売上に使う
  var bankRows = plBankParse_(L.bankVals, plBankRules_(L.ruleVals));
  var bank = plBankSummary_(bankRows, fy, L.settings, L.members);
  var otherFixed = plOtherFixed_(bank, L.settings, today);
  var st2 = {}; Object.keys(L.settings).forEach(function (k) { st2[k] = L.settings[k]; });
  st2.otherFixedMonthly = otherFixed.monthly;
  var ctx = { from: ym + "-01", to: asOf, members: L.members, att: L.att, calls: L.calls, appos: L.appos,
    pays: L.pays, shiftIdx: L.shiftIdx, settings: st2, kpiTargets: L.kpiTargets, mix: plMix_(L.appos, asOf, L.settings) };
  var sessions = plSessions_(ctx);
  // 稼働中の人＋その月に記録がある退職者
  var members0 = L.members.filter(function (m) { return m.active || sessions.some(function (s) { return s.pin === m.pin; }); })
    .map(function (m) { var r = plMemberMonth_(m, sessions, ym, asOf, ctx); r.active = m.active !== false; return r; });
  var members = members0;
  var team = plTeam_(members, ctx, ym, asOf);
  var appoRev = {};
  L.appos.forEach(function (a) { if (!a.cancelled && a.cls.counted) { var k = a.d.slice(0, 7); appoRev[k] = (appoRev[k] || 0) + a.cls.revenue; } });
  var ledger = plNormLedger_(L.ledgerVals, L.settings, today);
  var alloc = { unmatched: [] };
  if (Number(L.settings.bankAutoCollect) !== 0) alloc = plAllocateReceipts_(ledger, bankRows, L.projects, today);
  var bankSalesNet = {}, tr = plNum_(L.settings.taxRate) || 10;
  bank.months.forEach(function (m) { if (m.sales) bankSalesNet[m.ym] = Math.round(m.sales / (1 + tr / 100)); });
  var fySum = plFySummary_(ledger, fy, today, plNum_(L.settings.annualTarget), appoRev, bankSalesNet, L.settings.salesBasis || "自動");
  // v106: 今期の月ごとに「計算上の報酬（アプリの記録から）」と「銀行の振込」を突き合わせる
  var fyTo = today < fy.end ? today : fy.end;
  var fySessions = plSessions_({ from: fy.start, to: fyTo, members: L.members, att: L.att, calls: L.calls, appos: L.appos,
    pays: L.pays, shiftIdx: L.shiftIdx, settings: L.settings });
  var payCmp = plPayCompare_(L.members, fySessions, fy, fyTo, ctx, bank);
  var unknown = sessions.filter(function (s) { return !s.pin; });
  var warnings = [];
  members.forEach(function (m) { if (m.pay.missing && m.active) warnings.push(m.name + "：報酬設定がありません（⚙️設定で入力してください）"); });
  var mismatch = sessions.filter(function (s) { return s.pin && s.callAppos !== s.appos; });
  if (mismatch.length) warnings.push("架電結果の「アポ」とアポ報告の件数が合わない稼働が " + mismatch.length + " 件あります（アポ詳細の入力漏れ・二重登録の可能性。売上はアポ報告の件数で計算しています）");
  var noOut = sessions.filter(function (s) { return s.pin && s.hoursSrc !== "打刻" && s.calls > 0; });
  if (noOut.length) warnings.push("退勤の打刻がない稼働が " + noOut.length + " 件あります（時給の方は架電の最初〜最後で時間を推定しています）");
  if (unknown.length) warnings.push("名簿にない名前の記録が " + unknown.length + " 件あります（" +
    unknown.map(function (s) { return s.name; }).filter(function (v, i, a) { return a.indexOf(v) === i; }).slice(0, 5).join("・") + "）");
  var unreg = plUnregistered_(L.appos, fy.start);
  if (unreg.length) warnings.push("案件マスタに無い案件名が " + unreg.length + " 種類あります（📦案件で登録すると集計が正確になります）");
  if (bank.unclassified.length) warnings.push("銀行明細に区分が決まっていない取引が " + bank.unclassified.length + " 件あります（🏦銀行・収支で区分を決めてください）");
  if (bank.cash.total > 0) warnings.push("今期の現金引出が " + bank.cash.count + " 件・" + bank.cash.total + " 円あります（使い道を確認してください。収支には含めていません）");
  return {
    version: PL_VERSION, where: L.where, today: today, month: ym, asOf: asOf, settings: L.settings,
    pays: L.pays.map(function (p) { return { pin: p.pin, name: p.name, from: p.from, monthly: p.monthly, hourly: p.hourly, perAppo: p.perAppo, plannedDays: p.plannedDays, note: p.note }; }),
    members: members, team: team, daily: plDailySeries_(sessions, ym, asOf),
    sessions: sessions.filter(function (s) { return s.d.slice(0, 7) === ym; }),
    projects: plProjects_(L.projects, L.appos, today), unregistered: unreg,
    fy: fySum, ledger: ledger.sort(function (a, b) { return a.ym === b.ym ? (a.client < b.client ? -1 : 1) : (a.ym < b.ym ? 1 : -1); }),
    appoList: L.appos.filter(function (a) { return a.d.slice(0, 7) === ym; }).map(function (a) {
      return { row: a.row, d: a.d, reporter: a.reporter, project: a.project, company: a.company, pname: a.cls.pname,
        pid: a.cls.pid, counted: a.cls.counted, registered: a.cls.registered, revenue: a.cls.counted ? a.cls.revenue : 0,
        cancelled: a.cancelled, cancelNote: a.cancelNote || "" };
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.d === b.d ? b.row - a.row : (a.d < b.d ? 1 : -1); }),
    warnings: warnings, gasVersion: (typeof GAS_VERSION !== "undefined" ? GAS_VERSION : ""),
    bank: bank, bankRules: plBankRules_(L.ruleVals).map(function (r) { return { keyword: r.keyword, category: r.category, party: r.party, pin: r.pin, note: r.note }; }),
    unmatchedReceipts: alloc.unmatched, payCompare: payCmp, bankCats: PL_BANK_CATS.map(function (c) { return { name: c[0], dir: c[1], group: c[2] }; }),
    quality: plQuality_(sessions.filter(function (s) { return s.d.slice(0, 7) === ym; }), members),
    otherFixed: otherFixed,
    holidays: (function () { var o = []; for (var d = 1; d <= plDaysInMonth_(ym); d++) { var k = ym + "-" + plPad_(d), n = plHoliday_(k); if (n) o.push({ d: k, name: n }); } return o; })()
  };
}

// ── 書き込み（すべてロックの中で、IDで上書き） ──
function plWithLock_(fn) {
  var lock = null;
  try { lock = LockService.getScriptLock(); lock.waitLock(20000); } catch (e) { lock = null; }
  try { return fn(); } finally { try { if (lock) lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {} }
}
function plSetSettings(values) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.settings);
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), changed = [];
    Object.keys(values || {}).forEach(function (k) {
      var def = null; PL_SETTINGS_DEFAULT.forEach(function (r) { if (r[0] === k) def = r; });
      if (!def) return;
      var v = PL_TEXT_SETTINGS[k] ? String(values[k] == null ? "" : values[k]).trim() : plNum_(values[k]);
      if (k === "dueRule" && !v) v = "翌月末";
      if (k === "salesBasis" && ["自動", "台帳", "銀行"].indexOf(v) < 0) return;
      if (k === "otherFixedSource" && ["自動", "銀行", "手入力"].indexOf(v) < 0) return;
      if (k === "fiscalEndMonth" && (v < 1 || v > 12)) return;
      for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) {
        if (String(vals[i][0]) === k) { sh.getRange(i + 1, 3, 1, 3).setValues([[v, def[3], new Date()]]); changed.push(def[1]); return; }
      }
      sh.appendRow([k, def[1], v, def[3], new Date()]); changed.push(def[1]);
    });
    logAudit("収支設定の変更", "管理者", changed.join("・"), "");
    return { ok: true, changed: changed };
  });
}
function plSetPay(p) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.pay);
    var pin = rosterNormPin_(p.pin);
    if (!pin) throw new Error("PINがありません");
    var from = plMonthKey_(p.from);
    var line = ["'" + pin, FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || p.name || "", from ? "'" + from : "", plNum_(p.monthly) || "", plNum_(p.hourly) || "",
      plNum_(p.perAppo) || "", plNum_(p.plannedDays) || "", String(p.note || ""), new Date()];
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), found = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) {
      if (rosterNormPin_(vals[i][0]) === pin && plMonthKey_(vals[i][2]) === from) { found = i + 1; break; }
    }
    if (found) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, line.length).setValues([line]);
    else sh.appendRow(line);
    logAudit("報酬設定", "管理者", (FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || pin), "月給" + line[3] + "/時給" + line[4] + "/成果" + line[5] + (from ? "（" + from + "〜）" : ""));
    return { ok: true, pin: pin, from: from, updated: !!found };
  });
}
function plSetProject(p) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.project);
    var name = String(p.name || "").trim();
    if (!name) throw new Error("案件名を入れてください");
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var id = String(p.id || "").trim(), found = 0;
    if (id) { for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) if (String(vals[i][0]).trim() === id) { found = i + 1; break; } }
    if (!found) {
      // 同じ名前（名寄せ後）があればそれを更新する＝二重登録しない
      var nk = plNormName_(name);
      for (var j = 1; j < vals.length; j++) if (plNormName_(vals[j][1]) === nk) { found = j + 1; id = String(vals[j][0]).trim(); break; }
    }
    if (!id) id = "P-" + new Date().getTime().toString(36).toUpperCase() + "-" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1679616).toString(36).toUpperCase();
    var line = [id, name, String(p.aliases || ""), p.revenue === false || p.revenue === "しない" ? "しない" : "する",
      plNum_(p.price) || "", Math.round(plNum_(p.required)) || "", p.scope === "毎月" ? "毎月" : "総数",
      plDateKey_(p.start) ? "'" + plDateKey_(p.start) : "", plDateKey_(p.end) ? "'" + plDateKey_(p.end) : "",
      String(p.status || "進行中"), String(p.note || ""), new Date()];
    if (found) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, line.length).setValues([line]);
    else sh.appendRow(line);
    logAudit("案件マスタ", "管理者", name, found ? "更新" : "追加");
    return { ok: true, id: id, updated: !!found };
  });
}
function plDeleteRowById_(sheetName, id) {
  var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(vals[i][0]).trim() === String(id)) { sh.deleteRow(i + 1); return true; }
  }
  return false;
}
function plDeleteProject(id) {
  return plWithLock_(function () { var ok = plDeleteRowById_(PL_SHEET.project, id); logAudit("案件マスタ", "管理者", id, "削除"); return { ok: ok }; });
}
function plSetLedger(e) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.ledger);
    var st = plReadSettings_(ss);
    var ym = plMonthKey_(e.ym);
    if (!ym) throw new Error("計上月を入れてください");
    var amount = plNum_(e.amount);
    var rate = (e.taxRate === "" || e.taxRate === undefined || e.taxRate === null) ? plNum_(st.taxRate) : plNum_(e.taxRate);
    var billed = plNum_(e.billed) || Math.round(amount * (1 + rate / 100));
    var id = String(e.id || "").trim() || ("L-" + new Date().getTime().toString(36).toUpperCase() + "-" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1679616).toString(36).toUpperCase());   // v106: 同じミリ秒の登録でもIDが重ならない
    var inv = plDateKey_(e.invoiceDate), due = plDateKey_(e.dueDate), pdt = plDateKey_(e.paidDate);
    var paid = plNum_(e.paid);
    var stt = plLedgerStatus_(billed, paid, due || plDueDate_(inv, ym, st.dueRule), plTodayKey_());
    var line = [id, "'" + ym, String(e.client || ""), String(e.desc || ""), amount, rate, billed, inv ? "'" + inv : "",
      due ? "'" + due : "", paid || "", pdt ? "'" + pdt : "", stt.status, String(e.note || ""), String(e.source || "手入力"), new Date()];
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), found = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) if (String(vals[i][0]).trim() === id) { found = i + 1; break; }
    if (found) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, line.length).setValues([line]);
    else sh.appendRow(line);
    logAudit("売上台帳", "管理者", String(e.client || "") + " " + ym, (found ? "更新 " : "登録 ") + amount + "円");
    return { ok: true, id: id, updated: !!found, status: stt.status };
  });
}
// 入金の記録（入金済額に加算。請求額を超えたら請求額で止める）
function plLedgerPay(id, amount, date) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.ledger), st = plReadSettings_(ss);
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) {
      if (String(vals[i][0]).trim() !== String(id)) continue;
      var rows = plNormLedger_([PL_LEDGER_HEADER, vals[i]], st, plTodayKey_());
      var r = rows[0];
      var add = plNum_(amount) || r.outstanding;
      var paid = Math.min(r.billed, r.paid + add);
      var pd = plDateKey_(date) || plTodayKey_();
      var stt = plLedgerStatus_(r.billed, paid, r.dueDate, plTodayKey_());
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 10, 1, 3).setValues([[paid, "'" + pd, stt.status]]);
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 15).setValue(new Date());
      logAudit("入金の記録", "管理者", r.client + " " + r.ym, add + "円（累計" + paid + "円）");
      return { ok: true, paid: paid, status: stt.status };
    }
    throw new Error("その売上が見つかりません（ID: " + id + "）");
  });
}
function plDeleteLedger(id) {
  return plWithLock_(function () { var ok = plDeleteRowById_(PL_SHEET.ledger, id); logAudit("売上台帳", "管理者", id, "削除"); return { ok: ok }; });
}
// アポ実績から、その月の売上台帳を作る（案件ごと・何度押しても増えない）
function plLedgerFromAppos(month) {
  var ym = plMonthKey_(month) || plTodayKey_().slice(0, 7);
  var L = plLoad_();
  var drafts = plLedgerDraftFromAppos_(L.appos, ym);
  var made = 0, updated = 0;
  plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.ledger);
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), rowOf = {};
    for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) rowOf[String(vals[i][0]).trim()] = i + 1;
    var rate = plNum_(L.settings.taxRate) || 10, now = new Date(), app = [];
    drafts.forEach(function (x) {
      var billed = Math.round(x.amount * (1 + rate / 100));
      if (rowOf[x.id]) {
        // 金額と内容だけ更新（請求日・入金・メモは残す）
        var r = rowOf[x.id];
        sh.getRange(r, 3, 1, 5).setValues([[x.client, x.desc, x.amount, rate, billed]]);
        sh.getRange(r, 15).setValue(now);
        updated++;
      } else {
        app.push([x.id, "'" + ym, x.client, x.desc, x.amount, rate, billed, "", "", "", "", "未入金", "", "アポ実績", now]);
        made++;
      }
    });
    if (app.length) sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, app.length, PL_LEDGER_HEADER.length).setValues(app);
  });
  logAudit("売上台帳", "管理者", ym, "アポ実績から作成 " + made + "件／更新 " + updated + "件");
  return { ok: true, month: ym, made: made, updated: updated,
    total: drafts.reduce(function (a, x) { return a + x.amount; }, 0), items: drafts.length };
}
// 日次損益をシートに記録（日付×PIN で上書き＝冪等）
function plSnapshot(fromKey, toKey) {
  var today = plTodayKey_();
  var to = plDateKey_(toKey) || today, from = plDateKey_(fromKey) || (to.slice(0, 7) + "-01");
  var L = plLoad_();
  var sessions = plSessions_({ from: from, to: to, members: L.members, att: L.att, calls: L.calls, appos: L.appos,
    pays: L.pays, shiftIdx: L.shiftIdx, settings: L.settings });
  var unit = plNum_(L.settings.unitPrice) || 36000;
  var res = { rows: 0, added: 0, updated: 0, from: from, to: to };   // updated＝置き換えた古い行の数
  plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PL_SHEET.daily);
    var vals = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), keep = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) {
      var dk = plDateKey_(vals[i][0]);
      if (dk && dk >= from && dk <= to) { res.updated++; continue; }   // この期間の古い行は捨てて作り直す
      if (vals[i].some(function (c) { return c !== "" && c !== null; })) keep.push(vals[i]);
    }
    var now = new Date(), fresh = [];
    sessions.forEach(function (s) {
      if (!s.pin) return;
      fresh.push(["'" + s.d, "'" + s.pin, s.name, s.hours, s.hoursSrc, s.calls, s.appos, s.revAppos, s.nonRevAppos, s.revenue,
        s.costHourly, s.costCommission, s.costFixed, s.cost, s.profit, s.roi === null ? "" : s.roi,
        s.bepAppos === null ? "" : s.bepAppos, s.flags.join(" / "), now]);
    });
    res.rows = fresh.length; res.added = Math.max(0, fresh.length - res.updated);
    var all = keep.map(function (r) { var x = r.slice(0, PL_DAILY_HEADER.length); while (x.length < PL_DAILY_HEADER.length) x.push(""); return x; })
      .concat(fresh);
    all.sort(function (a, b) {
      var x = plDateKey_(String(a[0]).replace(/^'/, "")) + String(a[1]).replace(/^'/, ""), y = plDateKey_(String(b[0]).replace(/^'/, "")) + String(b[1]).replace(/^'/, "");
      return x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0;
    });
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, PL_DAILY_HEADER.length).clearContent();
    if (last > all.length + 1) sh.deleteRows(all.length + 2, last - all.length - 1);
    if (all.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, all.length, PL_DAILY_HEADER.length).setValues(all);
  });
  res.unitPrice = unit;
  return res;
}
// アポインター向け：案件名の候補（金額は返さない）
function plProjectNames() {
  var ss = plEnsure_();
  return plReadProjects_(ss).filter(function (p) { return p.status !== "停止"; })
    .map(function (p) { return { name: p.name, aliases: plSplitAliases_(p.aliases) }; });
}
// 夜間処理：当月分（月初5日までは前月分も）を記録し直す
function plNightly_() {
  var today = plTodayKey_(), extra = "";
  try { var st = plReadSettings_(plEnsure_()); if (st.bankFolderId) { var bi = plBankDriveImport_(st.bankFolderId); extra = "／銀行CSV 新規" + bi.added + "件"; } }
  catch (e) { extra = "／銀行CSVの取込失敗：" + String(e.message || e); }
  var from = (+today.slice(8, 10) <= 5) ? plAddDays_(today.slice(0, 7) + "-01", -1).slice(0, 7) + "-01" : today.slice(0, 7) + "-01";
  var r = plSnapshot(from, today);
  return r.rows + "行（" + from + "〜" + today + "）" + extra;
}
// 総合診断に「収支」の点検を加える
function plDiagChecks_() {
  var out = [];
  try {
    var ss = plEnsure_(), pays = plReadPays_(ss), st = plReadSettings_(ss), pj = plReadProjects_(ss);
    var miss = Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).filter(function (p) { return !pays.some(function (x) { return x.pin === p; }); });
    out.push(diagItem_("収支", "報酬設定", miss.length ? "warn" : "ok",
      miss.length ? "未設定: " + miss.map(function (p) { return FULL_NAME_MAP[p]; }).join("・") : Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).length + "名分あり",
      miss.length ? "「💹 収支・損益」→「⚙️設定」で月給・時給・成果報酬を入れてください" : ""));
    var ex = pj.filter(function (p) { return !p.revenue; }).map(function (p) { return p.name; });
    out.push(diagItem_("収支", "売上に換算しない案件", ex.length ? "ok" : "warn", ex.length ? ex.join("・") : "登録なし",
      ex.length ? "" : "「📦案件」で対象外の案件を登録してください"));
    out.push(diagItem_("収支", "単価・目標年商", "ok", "1件 " + plNum_(st.unitPrice) + "円／目標 " + plNum_(st.annualTarget) + "円", ""));
    var apw = admPwGet_();
    out.push(diagItem_("セキュリティ", "管理者パスワード（サーバーで照合）", apw ? "ok" : "ng",
      apw ? "設定済み（" + admWhen_(apw.setAt) + "）・登録端末 " + admDevices_().length + "台・保護している要求 " + Object.keys(ADMIN_ONLY_TYPES).length + "種類" : "未設定",
      apw ? "" : "ログイン画面の「🔐 管理者ログイン」→「初回設定」で15文字以上のパスワードを決めてください"));
    var kk0 = kanriKeysEnsure_();
    out.push(diagItem_("セキュリティ", "経営管理システムの連携キー", "ok", "スクリプトプロパティに保存（コードには無し）・作成 " + (kk0.at ? admWhen_(kk0.at) : ""), ""));
    out.push(diagItem_("シフト", "シフト申請の通知先", shiftNotifyList_().length ? "ok" : "ng", shiftNotifyList_().map(admMask_).join("・") || "なし", shiftNotifyList_().length ? "" : "CONFIG.NOTIFY.SHIFT_TO に管理者のメールを書いてください"));
  } catch (e) { out.push(diagItem_("収支", "収支シート", "ng", String(e), "管理者用スプレッドシートの共有を確認してください")); }
  return out;
}
// ── 要求の振り分け（doPost から呼ぶ） ──
function plHandle_(d) {
  if (!d || String(d.type || "").indexOf("pl_") !== 0) return null;
  function J(o) { return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(o)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); }
  try {
    var r;
    // 給与・売上を扱うため、案件名の候補（pl_projects）以外はパスコード必須
    if (d.type === "pl_auth") {
      if (d.action === "set") r = plAuthSet_(d.newKey, d.oldKey);
      else if (d.action === "check") { var ck = plAuthCheck_(d.key); r = { ok: ck.ok, need: ck.need || "", wrong: !!ck.wrong }; }
      else r = { set: !!plAuthState_().hash };
      return J({ status: "ok", handled: true, result: r });
    }
    if (d.type !== "pl_projects" && !ADM_REQ_OK_) {   // v109: 管理者ログインで保護（パスコードは不要）
      var au = plAuthCheck_(d.plKey);
      if (!au.ok) return J({ status: "ok", handled: true, auth: false, need: au.need, wrong: !!au.wrong });
    }
    switch (d.type) {
      case "pl_dashboard": r = plDashboard(d.month, d.today); break;
      case "pl_appo_fix": r = plAppoFix(d.fix || {}); break;
      case "pl_bank_import": r = plBankImport(d.rows || [], d.fileName || ""); break;
      case "pl_bank_rule_set": r = plBankRuleSet(d.rule || {}); break;
      case "pl_bank_rule_delete": r = plBankRuleDelete(d.keyword); break;
      case "pl_bank_tag": r = plBankTag(d.key, d.category, d.party); break;
      case "pl_bank_drive_import": r = plBankDriveImport_(d.folderId); break;
      case "pl_project_bulk": r = plProjectBulk(d.names || []); break;
      case "pl_settings_set": r = plSetSettings(d.values || {}); break;
      case "pl_pay_set": r = plSetPay(d.pay || {}); break;
      case "pl_project_set": r = plSetProject(d.project || {}); break;
      case "pl_project_delete": r = plDeleteProject(d.id); break;
      case "pl_ledger_set": r = plSetLedger(d.entry || {}); break;
      case "pl_ledger_pay": r = plLedgerPay(d.id, d.amount, d.date); break;
      case "pl_ledger_delete": r = plDeleteLedger(d.id); break;
      case "pl_ledger_from_appos": r = plLedgerFromAppos(d.month); break;
      case "pl_snapshot": r = plSnapshot(d.from, d.to); break;
      case "pl_projects": r = plProjectNames(); break;
      default: return J({ status: "ok", handled: false, type: d.type, gasVersion: GAS_VERSION });
    }
    return J({ status: "ok", handled: true, result: r });
  } catch (e) {
    return J({ status: "error", handled: true, message: String(e && e.message || e) });
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// アポの修正（案件名の付け直し・取消）。日付と法人名が一致する行だけ書き換える（行ずれ事故を防ぐ）
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plAppoFix(f) {
  return plWithLock_(function () {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID), sh = ss.getSheetByName("アポイント");
    if (!sh) throw new Error("「アポイント」シートがありません");
    var row = Math.round(plNum_(f.row));
    if (row < 2 || row > sh.getLastRow()) throw new Error("その行は見つかりません（" + row + "行目）。画面を更新してください");
    var lastC = Math.max(sh.getLastColumn(), 20);
    var head = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastC).getValues()[0], cc = -1;
    for (var c = 0; c < head.length; c++) if (/取消|キャンセル/.test(String(head[c] || ""))) { cc = c; break; }
    if (cc < 0) { cc = lastC; sh.getRange(1, cc + 1).setValue("取消"); }
    var r = sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, Math.max(lastC, cc + 1)).getValues()[0];
    if (f.d && plDateKey_(r[0]) !== plDateKey_(f.d)) throw new Error("行の内容が変わっています（日付が一致しません）。画面を更新してください");
    if (f.company !== undefined && String(r[5] || "").trim() !== String(f.company || "").trim())
      throw new Error("行の内容が変わっています（法人名が一致しません）。画面を更新してください");
    var did = [];
    if (f.project !== undefined && f.project !== null) {
      sh.getRange(row, 3).setValue(String(f.project || "").trim()); did.push("案件名→" + String(f.project || "").trim());
    }
    if (f.cancel !== undefined && f.cancel !== null) {
      sh.getRange(row, cc + 1).setValue(f.cancel ? ("取消 " + plTodayKey_() + (f.reason ? "：" + String(f.reason) : "")) : "");
      did.push(f.cancel ? "取消" : "取消を戻す");
    }
    logAudit("アポの修正", "管理者", String(r[5] || "") + "（" + plDateKey_(r[0]) + "）", did.join("・"));
    return { ok: true, row: row, done: did };
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// 経営データのパスコード（給与・売上を守る）
//   ・スクリプトプロパティにハッシュだけを保存（パスコードそのものは保存しない）
//   ・15分間に10回まちがえるとロック
//   ・忘れたら、エディタで plResetPasscode を実行して作り直す
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function plHashKey_(key, salt) {
  var raw = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, String(salt || "") + "|" + String(key || ""), Utilities.Charset.UTF_8);
  return raw.map(function (b) { return ("0" + (b & 255).toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join("");
}
function plAuthState_() {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  return { hash: p.getProperty("PL_PASS_HASH") || "", salt: p.getProperty("PL_PASS_SALT") || "" };
}
function plAuthCheck_(key) {
  var st = plAuthState_();
  if (!st.hash) return { ok: false, need: "setup" };
  var cache = null;
  try { cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); } catch (e) {}
  var fails = cache ? Number(cache.get("pl_auth_fail") || 0) : 0;
  if (fails >= 10) return { ok: false, need: "locked" };
  if (!key) return { ok: false, need: "key" };
  if (plHashKey_(key, st.salt) === st.hash) return { ok: true };
  if (cache) { try { cache.put("pl_auth_fail", String(fails + 1), 900); } catch (e2) {} }
  return { ok: false, need: fails + 1 >= 10 ? "locked" : "key", wrong: true };
}
function plAuthSet_(newKey, oldKey) {
  var k = String(newKey == null ? "" : newKey);
  if (k.length < 6) return { ok: false, message: "パスコードは6文字以上にしてください" };
  var st = plAuthState_();
  if (st.hash) {
    var c = plAuthCheck_(oldKey);
    if (!c.ok) return { ok: false, need: c.need, message: c.need === "locked" ? "まちがいが続いたため15分間ロックしています" : "今のパスコードが違います" };
  }
  var salt = Utilities.getUuid();
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  p.setProperty("PL_PASS_SALT", salt);
  p.setProperty("PL_PASS_HASH", plHashKey_(k, salt));
  logAudit("経営パスコード", "管理者", st.hash ? "変更" : "初回設定", "");
  return { ok: true, changed: !!st.hash };
}
// エディタで実行：パスコードを忘れたとき（次に管理画面を開くと、新しく決める画面になります）
function plResetPasscode() {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  p.deleteProperty("PL_PASS_HASH"); p.deleteProperty("PL_PASS_SALT");
  Logger.log("経営パスコードを初期化しました。管理画面の「💹 収支・損益」で新しく決めてください。");
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v106: 銀行明細 → 今期の収支（現金ベース）・入金の自動消込・アポインターへの支払
//   ・CSV（PayPay銀行の形式ほか）を画面で読み込み、1行ずつ「キー」で保存（何度取り込んでも重複しない）
//   ・「銀行の仕訳ルール」（摘要に含むキーワード → 区分・相手）で自動で区分する
//   ・区分できない取引は画面で区分を決める → ルールとして残るので、次回からは自動
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var PL_BANK_HEADER = ["キー", "日付", "時刻", "取引順番号", "摘要", "出金", "入金", "残高", "メモ", "手動の区分", "手動の相手", "取込日時", "取込ファイル"];
var PL_RULE_HEADER = ["キーワード（摘要に含む）", "区分", "相手", "PIN", "メモ", "更新日時"];
// [区分, 収入/支出/対象外/未分類, まとめ]

  ["ジヤパンミライズ", "売上入金", "ジャパンミライズ(株)", "", ""],
  ["フアンコミユニケ-シヨンズ", "売上入金", "(株)ファンコミュニケーションズ", "", ""],
  ["ガイコクジンシエンセンタ-", "売上入金", "(同)外国人支援センター", "", ""],
  ["カタランカ", "売上入金", "(同)カタランカ", "", ""],
  ["エムエスアジア", "売上入金", "MSA", "", "エムエスアジア"],
  ["オクトパスエナジ-", "売上入金", "TGオクトパスエナジー(株)", "", ""],
  ["グルメキネヤ", "売上入金", "(株)グルメ杵屋", "", ""]
].concat((function () {
  // v108: 人の振込名義の仕訳は CONFIG.BANK_PERSON_RULES から作る。PIN は名簿の稼働中の人だけに付ける
  //       （退職した人のPINを付けない＝PINを再利用しても、前の人の振込が新しい人に付かない）
  var byName = {};
  CONFIG.ROSTER.forEach(function (m) { if (!m.status || m.status === "稼働") byName[m.name] = m.pin; });
  return CONFIG.BANK_PERSON_RULES.map(function (r) {
    var pin = (r[1] === "アポインター報酬" && byName[r[2]]) ? byName[r[2]] : "";
    return [r[0], r[1], r[2], pin, r[3] || ""];
  });
})());
function plBankCat_(name) { for (var i = 0; i < PL_BANK_CATS.length; i++) if (PL_BANK_CATS[i][0] === name) return PL_BANK_CATS[i]; return null; }
// 摘要の照合用：全角/半角・長音・小さい文字・空白の違いを吸収（銀行は「ョ」を「ヨ」、「ー」を「－」で出すため）
function plKanaKey_(s) {
  var t = String(s == null ? "" : s);
  try { t = t.normalize("NFKC"); } catch (e) { t = plZen2Han_(t); }
  t = t.toUpperCase().replace(/[\s\u3000]/g, "").replace(/[-‐‑–—―ーｰ−]/g, "ー");
  var small = { "ァ": "ア", "ィ": "イ", "ゥ": "ウ", "ェ": "エ", "ォ": "オ", "ッ": "ツ", "ャ": "ヤ", "ュ": "ユ", "ョ": "ヨ", "ヮ": "ワ", "ヵ": "カ", "ヶ": "ケ" };
  return t.replace(/[ァィゥェォッャュョヮヵヶ]/g, function (c) { return small[c]; });
}
function plBankRules_(vals) {
  var out = [];
  (vals || []).forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!i) return;
    var kw = String(r[0] || "").trim(); if (!kw) return;
    var cat = String(r[1] || "").trim(); if (!plBankCat_(cat)) cat = "未分類";
    var rpin = r[3] === "" || r[3] === null || r[3] === undefined ? "" : rosterNormPin_(r[3]);
    if (rpin && !pinOwnerOk_(rpin, r[2])) rpin = "";   // v108: PINを引き継いだ後も、前の方への振込を新しい方に付けない
    out.push({ keyword: kw, key: plKanaKey_(kw), category: cat, party: String(r[2] || "").trim(),
      pin: rpin, note: String(r[4] || ""), row: i + 1 });
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.key.length - a.key.length; });   // 長いキーワードを優先
  return out;
}
// 1取引 → 区分（手動の区分 ＞ ルール（最長一致）＞ 未分類）
function plBankClassify_(desc, rules, manualCat, manualParty) {
  if (manualCat && plBankCat_(manualCat)) return { category: manualCat, party: manualParty || "", pin: "", by: "手動" };
  var k = plKanaKey_(desc);
  for (var i = 0; i < rules.length; i++) {
    if (rules[i].key && k.indexOf(rules[i].key) >= 0)
      return { category: rules[i].category, party: manualParty || rules[i].party, pin: rules[i].pin, by: "ルール", rule: rules[i].keyword };
  }
  return { category: "未分類", party: manualParty || "", pin: "", by: "" };
}
// 取込：CSVの1行 → 保存用（キーで重複を判定）
function plBankKey_(r) {
  return [r.date, r.time || "", r.seq || "", plNum_(r.out), plNum_(r.inc), r.bal === "" || r.bal === undefined ? "" : plNum_(r.bal)].join("|");
}
function plBankParse_(vals, rules) {
  var out = [];
  (vals || []).forEach(function (r, i) {
    if (!i) return;
    var d = plDateKey_(r[1]); if (!d) return;
    var desc = String(r[4] || "");
    var c = plBankClassify_(desc, rules, String(r[9] || "").trim(), String(r[10] || "").trim());
    out.push({ row: i + 1, key: String(r[0] || ""), d: d, time: String(r[2] || ""), seq: String(r[3] || ""), desc: desc,
      out: plNum_(r[5]), inc: plNum_(r[6]), bal: (r[7] === "" || r[7] === null) ? null : plNum_(r[7]), memo: String(r[8] || ""),
      category: c.category, party: c.party, pin: c.pin, by: c.by, rule: c.rule || "" });
  });
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d === b.d ? (a.time + a.seq < b.time + b.seq ? -1 : 1) : (a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1); });
  return out;
}
// 振込日 → 何月分の報酬か（例：10/1 の振込 → 5日前 9/26 → 9月分）
function plPayWorkMonth_(d, offsetDays) { return plAddDays_(d, -Math.max(0, Math.round(plNum_(offsetDays)))).slice(0, 7); }
// 今期の収支（現金ベース）
function plBankSummary_(rows, fy, settings, members) {
  var months = fy.months.map(function (ym) { return { ym: ym, inc: 0, out: 0, net: 0, sales: 0, labor: 0, appointer: 0, expense: 0, other: 0, cashOut: 0, unclassified: 0, bal: null }; });
  var mi = {}; months.forEach(function (m, i) { mi[m.ym] = i; });
  var cats = {}, parties = {}, pay = {}, unc = [], cash = { total: 0, count: 0 };
  var first = null, last = null, opening = null;
  var offset = settings && settings.payOffsetDays !== undefined ? settings.payOffsetDays : 5;
  var byName = {}; (members || []).forEach(function (m) { byName[plPersonKey_(m.name)] = m.pin; });
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    if (r.d < fy.start || r.d > fy.end) return;
    var m = months[mi[r.d.slice(0, 7)]]; if (!m) return;
    if (!first) { first = r; opening = r.bal !== null ? r.bal - r.inc + r.out : null; }
    last = r; if (r.bal !== null) m.bal = r.bal;
    var cat = plBankCat_(r.category) || plBankCat_("未分類"), dir = cat[1], grp = cat[2];
    var amt = r.inc - r.out;
    if (!cats[cat[0]]) cats[cat[0]] = { name: cat[0], dir: dir, group: grp, inc: 0, out: 0, count: 0, byMonth: {} };
    var C = cats[cat[0]]; C.inc += r.inc; C.out += r.out; C.count++; C.byMonth[m.ym] = (C.byMonth[m.ym] || 0) + amt;
    if (dir === "対象外") { m.cashOut += r.out - r.inc; if (/現金/.test(cat[0])) { cash.total += r.out - r.inc; cash.count++; } return; }
    if (dir === "未分類") { m.unclassified += amt; unc.push(r); }
    m.inc += r.inc; m.out += r.out;
    if (cat[0] === "売上入金") {
      m.sales += r.inc - r.out;
      var pk = r.party || "(相手不明)";
      if (!parties[pk]) parties[pk] = { party: pk, total: 0, count: 0, last: "", byMonth: {} };
      parties[pk].total += r.inc - r.out; parties[pk].count++; parties[pk].last = r.d;
      parties[pk].byMonth[m.ym] = (parties[pk].byMonth[m.ym] || 0) + r.inc - r.out;
    } else if (grp === "人件費") {
      m.labor += r.out - r.inc;
      if (cat[0] === "アポインター報酬") {
        m.appointer += r.out - r.inc;
        var nm = r.party || "(名前不明)", pin = r.pin || byName[plPersonKey_(nm)] || "";
        var key = pin || nm, wm = plPayWorkMonth_(r.d, offset);
        if (!pay[key]) pay[key] = { pin: pin, name: nm, total: 0, count: 0, byWorkMonth: {}, list: [] };
        pay[key].total += r.out - r.inc; pay[key].count++;
        pay[key].byWorkMonth[wm] = (pay[key].byWorkMonth[wm] || 0) + r.out - r.inc;
        pay[key].list.push({ d: r.d, amount: r.out - r.inc, workMonth: wm });
      }
    } else if (dir === "支出") m.expense += r.out - r.inc;
    else if (dir === "収入") m.other += r.inc - r.out;
  });
  var T = { inc: 0, out: 0, sales: 0, labor: 0, appointer: 0, expense: 0, other: 0, unclassified: 0 };
  var bal = opening;
  months.forEach(function (m) {
    m.net = m.inc - m.out;
    if (m.bal === null) m.bal = bal; else bal = m.bal;
    T.inc += m.inc; T.out += m.out; T.sales += m.sales; T.labor += m.labor; T.appointer += m.appointer; T.expense += m.expense; T.other += m.other; T.unclassified += m.unclassified;
  });
  T.net = T.inc - T.out;
  var rate = plNum_(settings && settings.taxRate) || 10;
  T.salesExTax = Math.round(T.sales / (1 + rate / 100));
  var catList = PL_BANK_CATS.map(function (c) { return cats[c[0]]; }).filter(function (x) { return x; });
  return {
    from: first ? first.d : "", to: last ? last.d : "", rows: rows.filter(function (r) { return r.d >= fy.start && r.d <= fy.end; }).length,
    allRows: rows.length, opening: opening, closing: last ? last.bal : null, months: months, totals: T, cats: catList,
    parties: Object.keys(parties).map(function (k) { return parties[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.total - a.total; }),
    payments: Object.keys(pay).map(function (k) { return pay[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.total - a.total; }),
    cash: cash,
    unclassified: unc.map(function (r) { return { key: r.key, d: r.d, desc: r.desc, inc: r.inc, out: r.out }; }),
    recent: rows.filter(function (r) { return r.d >= fy.start && r.d <= fy.end; }).slice(-400).reverse()
      .map(function (r) { return { key: r.key, d: r.d, desc: r.desc, inc: r.inc, out: r.out, bal: r.bal, category: r.category, party: r.party, by: r.by }; })
  };
}
// 入金の自動消込：取引先ごとに、入金日より前に計上した古い請求から順に充当（同じ入金を二重に使わない）
function plPartyKey_(name, projects) {
  var p = plMatchProject_(name, projects || []);
  return p ? "P:" + p.id : "N:" + plNormName_(name);
}
function plSameParty_(a, b) {
  if (a === b) return true;
  if (a.indexOf("N:") !== 0 || b.indexOf("N:") !== 0) return false;
  var x = a.slice(2), y = b.slice(2);
  return x.length >= 3 && y.length >= 3 && (x.indexOf(y) >= 0 || y.indexOf(x) >= 0);
}
function plAllocateReceipts_(ledger, bankRows, projects, today) {
  var groups = [];
  (ledger || []).forEach(function (r) {
    r.bankPaid = 0; r.manualPaid = r.paid;
    var k = plPartyKey_(r.client, projects), g = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) if (plSameParty_(groups[i].key, k)) { g = groups[i]; break; }
    if (!g) { g = { key: k, rows: [] }; groups.push(g); }
    g.rows.push(r);
  });
  groups.forEach(function (g) { g.rows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.ym === b.ym ? (a.invoiceDate < b.invoiceDate ? -1 : 1) : (a.ym < b.ym ? -1 : 1); }); });
  var unmatched = {};
  (bankRows || []).filter(function (r) { return r.category === "売上入金" && r.inc > 0; }).forEach(function (r) {
    var k = plPartyKey_(r.party || r.desc, projects), g = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) if (plSameParty_(groups[i].key, k)) { g = groups[i]; break; }
    var left = r.inc;
    if (g) {
      g.rows.forEach(function (x) {
        if (left <= 0 || x.ym + "-01" > r.d) return;          // 入金日より後に計上した請求には充てない
        var room = x.billed - x.bankPaid;
        if (room <= 0) return;
        var use = Math.min(room, left); x.bankPaid += use; left -= use;
        if (!x.bankLast || r.d > x.bankLast) x.bankLast = r.d;
      });
    }
    if (left > 0) {
      var nm = r.party || r.desc;
      if (!unmatched[nm]) unmatched[nm] = { party: nm, amount: 0, count: 0, last: "" };
      unmatched[nm].amount += left; unmatched[nm].count++; if (r.d > unmatched[nm].last) unmatched[nm].last = r.d;
    }
  });
  (ledger || []).forEach(function (r) {
    // 手入力の入金額と銀行の充当額の大きいほう（同じ入金を手入力していても二重にならない）
    var paid = Math.min(r.billed, Math.max(r.manualPaid, r.bankPaid));
    if (paid !== r.paid) {
      r.paid = paid;
      if (r.bankPaid >= r.manualPaid && r.bankLast) r.paidDate = r.bankLast;
      var st = plLedgerStatus_(r.billed, paid, r.dueDate, today);
      r.status = st.status; r.outstanding = st.outstanding; r.overdueDays = st.overdueDays;
    }
    r.paidBy = r.bankPaid > r.manualPaid ? "銀行" : (r.manualPaid > 0 ? "手入力" : "");
  });
  return { unmatched: Object.keys(unmatched).map(function (k) { return unmatched[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.amount - a.amount; }) };
}
// データ収集状況（アポインターごと・その月）
function plQuality_(sessions, members) {
  var by = {};
  (members || []).forEach(function (m) { by[m.pin] = { pin: m.pin, name: m.name, days: 0, inp: 0, out: 0, calls: 0, apo: 0, proj: 0, score: null }; });
  (sessions || []).forEach(function (s) {
    if (!s.pin || !by[s.pin] || !s.q) return;
    var b = by[s.pin]; b.days++;
    if (s.q.inp) b.inp++; if (s.q.out) b.out++; if (s.q.calls) b.calls++; if (s.q.apo) b.apo++; if (s.q.proj) b.proj++;
  });
